Versi lama memakai SATU sudut = sudut gameplay persis sepanjang cutscene, hanya titik fokusnya yang berpindah. Shot BARU: `establish` (kota nyaris dari atas, heli sebutir berbeacon = SKALA), `flare` (heli MENDONGAK menahan laju), `land` (ketukan pendaratan; shake 1.4→4.2). Tambahan hidup: DEBU downwash rotor di dek (pool ground-puff yang sudah ada, tanpa aset baru), BEACON berkedip (`beaconM.emissiveIntensity` = uniform, tanpa recompile), heli BERMANUVER (bank / dongak flare / tunduk saat pergi). Helper: `setShotCam(az,dist,h)` / `shotCam(A,B,k)` / tabel `SHOT` / `flyHeli(k)` / `followHeliPivot()` / `pulseBeacon(t)` / `washRoof(dt,kuat)`. **PASS KEDUA 2026-07-27 (permintaan user: "transisi antar scene masih terlihat kasar dan terlihat tiba-tiba" + "gerak kamera 360 derajat ketika helicopter di atas gedung terlihat berlebihan"):** (a) **SATU BUSUR SEARAH 75°, bukan orbit** — papan lama menyapu +105° lalu BERBALIK −195° (~300° dgn pembalikan di tengah = terbaca "mengelilingi gedung"); azimut tiap shot kini SIMPANGAN dari sudut kamera gameplay (`CAM_OFF_DEFAULT` renderer.js, helper `az(delta)` — JANGAN salin angkanya) yang menyusut 75°→0°; (b) **`settleCam(dt, snap)` MEREDAM kamera & kabut** — `setShotCam` kini menulis ke `camTarget`, `camOff` mengejarnya (konstanta waktu `CAM_SETTLE` ~0,28 dtk) sehingga tiap patahan di batas shot luruh mulus + kamera punya BOBOT; dipanggil SEKALI di akhir `updateMode` (dilewati bila `finishIntro` baru jalan — ia sudah memulihkan kabut Stage 1); (c) **`settlePivot(dt,x,y,z)` MEREDAM titik fokus HANYA selama avatar tersembunyi** (`flare`/`hover`) — peralihan pivot "menempel heli di langit" → "titik turun di atap" dulu terjadi dalam SATU frame = lompatan ~59 unit, sentakan terbesar versi lama; sejak `descend` pivot = posisi avatar jadi ditulis PERSIS (peredaman akan membuat avatar tertinggal dari talinya); (d) **`SHOT.establishEnd`** = titik MULAI shot 2 (dulu shot 1 hanyut +6° lalu shot 2 memulai dari nilai establish = patahan 6° dalam satu frame); (e) **shot penutup `door` = LITERAL `CAM_OFF_DEFAULT`** (azimut/jarak/tinggi) → serah-terima ke Stage 1 tanpa jentikan 90°; (f) **TIRAI HITAM `dom.setCineFade`** (`FADE_IN_SEC` 1,1 / `FADE_OUT_SEC` 0,9 / `FADE_BACK_SEC` 0,7): buka dari hitam di frame pertama, tutup ke hitam SEBELUM `setScene(stage1)`, buka lagi setelah tutorial tampil. Dijaga assert `INTRO KAMERA`/`INTRO TIRAI` (busur 40-150° tanpa pembalikan, lompatan per-frame sudut <1,2° / tinggi <35 / pivot <14 / kabut <60, shot penutup = sudut gameplay, tirai hitam saat scene berganti). **KONTRAK NARASI dijaga assert `INTRO NARASI 1-4` (smoke): datang naik HELIKOPTER → TURUN di atap → helikopter PERGI → MASUK gedung — diperiksa sebagai URUTAN, jadi re-cut yang menghilangkan salah satunya langsung merah.** Fase: establish→fly→flare→hover→rope→descend→land→ropeUp→heliLeave→walk→enter→wait**: `introScene` (id `campaign-intro`) + `beginIntro()`, `warmupIntro()`, `introDebug()`, **`introMetrics()` (2026-07-19 — `{hoverY, landmarks:{monas,gbk,bundaranHI}}` utk smoke)**, **`cityDebug()` (2026-07-20 — `{roads, towers, crowns, houses, rukos, carDots, lampHeads}` utk smoke kota Jakarta)**. **`HOVER_Y` DITURUNKAN 2× (128 → 64 → 48, 2026-07-19 permintaan user) — heli menggantung rendah di atas deck; tali/descend/kamera semua turunan dari konstanta ini.** **LANDMARK JAKARTA (2026-07-19, permintaan user + foto referensi — `buildLandmarks(parent, col)`): MONAS di taman Medan Merdeka (rumput 600², pelataran batu + cawan marmer DISOROT [emissive lembut 0.5 — Monas malam] + obelisk menjulang + LIDAH API emas emissive 0.85 ≤ EMISSIVE_MAX, **+ RING JALAN Medan Merdeka keliling taman ber-trafik tipis 2026-07-20**) di TIMUR-LAUT hero (tepat arah pandang kamera SW→NE saat hover/turun tali) + STADION GBK (mangkuk OVAL = shell silinder terbuka dlm grup scale-x 1.35 + ring kanopi + lapangan hijau + lintasan + 4 menara lampu sudut) di BARAT-DAYA (terlihat selama fly-in); Bundaran HI = `buildRoundabout`. Keduanya punya zona eksklusi `LM_MONAS`/`LM_GBK` yang dilewati loop gedung/rumah/pohon.** **LATAR KOTA JAKARTA (DIROMBAK 2026-07-20, permintaan user + foto udara Sudirman-Thamrin malam; sebelumnya 2026-07-18 "kota renggang"):** `buildCity(parent)` bikin atap ini = puncak SATU menara di tengah kota yang BISA DIKENALI sbg Jakarta, semua di `CITY_GROUND` −520 di bawah deck — **JALAN PROTOKOL** (`SUDIRMAN` lewat sisi timur GBK→Bundaran HI, `THAMRIN` Bundaran HI→Medan Merdeka, `TOLL` Gatot Subroto menyilang di Semanggi; `buildRoads`/`dressRoad` = aspal bermarka lajur + median hijau + lampu jalan dua sisi + **ARUS LALU LINTAS**: pasangan titik lampu MERAH/PUTIH ala foto long-exposure, InstancedMesh per warna via kolektor `col` + helper generik `instBoxes`) + **simpang susun SEMANGGI** (`buildSemanggi` — dek RING LAYANG + tepi menyala hangat + kolom + trafik memutar) + **BUNDARAN HI** (`buildRoundabout(parent, col)` — ring aspal + rumput + KOLAM menyala + Monumen Selamat Datang di kolam + semburan air mancur + cincin lampu + trafik) + **hamparan KAMPUNG** (`buildKampung` — ~820 rumah kecil ber-orientasi acak SATU InstancedMesh + taburan lampu hangat; tanah kota `cityTex` juga bertabur cahaya hunian) + **deretan RUKO/TOKO** (`buildRukoRows` — blok 2-4 lantai fasad kasar `makeFacadeTex(4)` + STRIP SIGNBOARD menyala amber/putih/hazard/teal menghadap jalan) + **SEKOLAH** (`buildSchool` — gedung kelas putih bentuk L beratap genteng + lapangan upacara + koridor menyala + tiang BENDERA MERAH-PUTIH, timur Thamrin) + **GEDUNG TINGGI TIDAK BANYAK** (permintaan user: menara jangkung HANYA di koridor Sudirman-Thamrin [canyon <300 unit, prob 0.5, jendela menyala campuran hangat/putih `makeJktLitTex` + **MAHKOTA ATAP menyala** amber/putih/teal utk h>280], luar koridor blok rendah jarang prob 0.14; `fillBuildingInstances` + `instBoxes` crowns) + TAMAN hijau + **POHON** (`buildTrees` — separuh BERBARIS di jalur hijau tepi jalan protokol, sisanya tersebar) + kanal di utara; helper `distSeg`/`distMainRoad` = zona eksklusi jalan utk semua scatter; `buildIntroSky(parent)` = **LANGIT MALAM (dirombak 2026-07-27)**: kubah IKUT kamera (`positionIntroSky`, `SKY_RADIUS` **2900** — diturunkan dari 3400 karena shot `establish` menaruh kamera 620 unit di atas pivot sehingga sisi terjauh kubah jatuh di 4294 > `camera.far` 4000 = **kubah terpotong**; kini ~3794, dijaga assert), teksturnya dilukis SEKALI: gradien malam → **pita horizon berwarna `FOG_HEX` PERSIS** + ~686 **BINTANG** dari zenit sampai tepat di atas horizon (dulu 200 & hanya di 35% teratas = tak pernah masuk frame) + pita Bimasakti + pijar lampu kota di horizon. `buildMoon(dome)` = **BULAN** + halo aditif, anak kubah (benda langit di tak-hingga), `MOON_AZ/EL/DIST` 212°/3.4°/2650 — **hanya 2 objek gambar** (dijaga assert `domeChildren === 2`); sisanya nol biaya karena dilukis ke tekstur. **Tinggi shot `flare`/`hover`/`rope`/`wide` diturunkan** supaya langit benar-benar masuk frame: letterbox `CINE_BAR_FRAC` 0.13 memakan 6.5° dari setengah-FOV 25°, jadi sebuah shot memperlihatkan langit hanya bila tinggi/jarak < tan(18.5°)≈0.33 — sebelum ini SELURUH cutscene 0° langit. **KABUT per-shot**: `SHOT[k][3..4]` = `fogNear/fogFar` yang di-lerp `shotCam` (uniform = gratis); `fogFar` SELALU ≤ `CITY_FILL` sehingga dataran kosong + tepi hamparan mustahil terlihat, dan `scene.fog.color` = `FOG_HEX` = pita horizon kubah sehingga tanah LARUT ke langit tanpa garis sambungan. **`CITY_FILL` 2550 / `CITY_PLANE` 6600**: isian kota (menara/kampung/pohon) disebar sampai pinggir (dulu berhenti di dz −1150 / |dx| 1980 = cincin luar KOSONG) — semua masuk InstancedMesh yang SUDAH ada → **+0 draw call, +0 PointLight**; menara di cincin luar diperjarang (0.06) & tetap blok rendah. `introSkyDebug()` = {stars, hasMoon, moonRel/Az/El/Dist, skyRadius, skyToCam, fogHex/Near/Far, horizonHex, fill, plane, barFrac, domeChildren} utk smoke. Selama cutscene: kubah KOBARAN API global disembunyikan (`decor.skyDome.visible=false`), fog global disimpan `savedFog`, bara/abu `setEmbersVisible(false)` — SEMUA dipulihkan `finishIntro`. **`warmupIntro()` (dipanggil `main.js` SETELAH `warmupAll`, MASIH di balik loading): render scene intro dari SEMUA sudut kamera cutscene (fly jauh + hover + jalan + **satu view MENATAP LANGIT ke arah bulan** — `renderer.compile` hanya menyiapkan program, tekstur baru terunggah saat benar-benar tergambar) → SELURUH buffer/tekstur kota+langit terunggah ke GPU → cutscene TANPA lag/freeze/stutter (permintaan user).** Diputar **hanya pada start campaign BARU** (bukan Continue `opts.stage>1`/restart/cheat) SEBELUM Stage 1 (wiring di `main.js`: `playIntro = mode==='campaign' && !(opts.stage>1)` → `setScene(introScene)` lalu `beginIntro()` setelah avatar/senjata ter-init, SEBELUM `warmupAll` supaya heli/tali ikut ter-compile). Scene NON-gameplay (semua hook gameplay no-op, `cinematicActive` membekukan kontrol+input KECUALI Esc). **AUTO-PLAY (2026-07-17): cutscene diputar OTOMATIS begitu game dimuat — TANPA layar tutorial "Click to Start" lebih dulu (`beginIntro` `setPaused(false)` + SEMBUNYIKAN `#blocker`); MUSIK MENU dihentikan + DERU HELI (`playLoopSFX(sfxHeli)`) + TOMBOL SKIP baru dijalankan di frame PERTAMA `updateMode` via flag `cine.live` (2026-07-20 bug fix — `beginIntro` dipanggil main.js MASIH di balik layar loading [sebelum warmupAll/hideLoading], jadi suara heli & tombol skip dulu bocor muncul saat loading; Space saat loading aman: callback belum terdaftar → `triggerCutsceneSkip` no-op); layar tutorial baru ditampilkan `finishIntro` SETELAH cutscene (`setScene(stage1)` + `setPaused(true)` + `hidePauseMenu` + tampilkan blocker) — pointer belum pernah terkunci selama auto-play, jadi klik blocker = start awal (bukan resume) → gameplay Stage 1.** Dua adegan, mesin BERBASIS TIMER (fase `fly`→`hover`→`rope`→`descend`→`ropeUp`→`heliLeave`→`walk`→`enter`→`wait`; durasi dari **`CFG.campaign.intro`**): **SCENE 1 + kedatangan DIGABUNG jadi SATU fase `fly` (transisi mulus 2026-07-17)** — HELIKOPTER (`entities/helicopter.js`) **MENYUSURI langit MALAM** menempuh SATU lintasan LURUS `FLY_START`→hover dgn easeOut (cruise → MELAMBAT berhenti mulus di titik menggantung; TANPA belok arah / lompatan kecepatan — dulu `fly` linear lalu `approach` easeOut bikin patah), bank `rotation.z` diluruskan saat tiba; kamera MENGIKUTI heli selama `flySec` lalu BLEND MULUS (smoothstep) ke framing tali selama `approachSec` terakhir → `hover` MENGGANTUNG → `rope` TALI menjuntai (silinder tumbuh scale.y) → `descend` character player TURUN dari tali → `walk` BERJALAN ke PINTU → `enter` MASUK → **doorDelaySec (2 dtk) → `setScene(stage1Scene)`**. **MEKANIK KAMERA (2026-07-17 rev): DIKENDALIKAN lewat PIVOT — `camera` (pivot logika) = posisi avatar DAN fokus kamera RENDER (viewCam MENGIKUTI pivot via dead-zone `followViewCam`; TIDAK pakai `setCineFocus`/cineFocus = null, supaya heli terbang & avatar turun terbuntuti KETAT).** SCENE 1: `camFollowHeli` menaruh pivot = posisi heli (Y digeser `AIR_DROP` ke bawah → heli di ATAS layar, langit sekeliling; kamera menyusuri langit bersama heli). SCENE 2: `descend` menurunkan `camera.position.y` (avatar TURUN, kamera pan turun mengikutinya — roof naik ke frame), `walk`/`enter` menggeser `camera.position.xz` (siklus jalan terpicu kecepatan); **`descend` memanggil `setAvatarRappel(true, k, yawKePintu)` tiap frame → POSE FAST-ROPE (playerAvatar.js), dilepas `setAvatarRappel(false)` + `addCamShake` hentakan pendaratan saat menyentuh atap**; `aimPoint` (input.js) di-override tiap frame (`faceAvatar`) supaya avatar MENGHADAP pintu; `avatarGroup.visible` di-toggle (tersembunyi saat masih di heli, tampil saat mulai turun, hilang saat masuk gedung). `setCineBars` letterbox. **Dunia ATAP `buildRoof()` (jauh, x≈150000, deck y=0): deck beton + parapet keliling + BULKHEAD berpintu (bukaan gelap = jalan turun ke Stage 1) + lampu pintu amber + TANGKI AIR kayu ikonik + unit HVAC + ventilasi — semua Lambert/Basic token PAL**; `enter()` juga memanggil `ensureCampaignWorlds` (stage1 `ensureWorld`) supaya Stage 1-4 sudah dibangun untuk warmup + transisi. `finishIntro()` matikan sinematik + **`disposeIntroWorld()`** (buang atap+heli+tali, reset guard — cutscene sekali-jalan) → Stage 1. `introDebug()` (`{phase, active, heliX/Y/Z, avatarShown, pivotX/Y/Z, door, drop, roofY, eyeH}`) utk smoke.
- **campaign/cutscenes/tankBossIntro.js** — **CUTSCENE TANK-BOSS ("penjemputan gagal")** — dipisah dari `stages/stage4/index.js` 2026-07-19, **DIROMBAK TOTAL 2026-07-27 (permintaan user: "buat agar jauh lebih dramatis, jauh lebih cinematic ... SEPERTI FILM BOX OFFICE")**. `createTankBossIntro(deps)` → pengontrol `{update, start, reset, skip, currentHeli, camOffset, cineDebug, isHeliSpawned, isActive, isDone}`; `deps` = geometri stage 4 (`SQ`/`HELI_POS`/`BOSS_POS`/`WRECK_CLEAR`/`S4_START`) + `blockers` + `openGate` + `setTank(t)`. Milik modul ini: siklus HELI penjemput (`heliArrives` = buka gerbang + `spawnHelicopter` di pusat alun + blocker; `reset` = dispose + cabut blocker + batalkan sinematik) + mesin shot. **PAPAN 11 SHOT (durasi tiap shot dari `CFG.campaign.tankIntro`):** `open` (player berhenti melangkah, letterbox masuk, dorongan halus) → `survey` (CRANE naik & mundur menyapu alun-alun, fokus meluncur player→heli) → **`tremor` (TELEGRAF BARU: CUT ke sudut RENDAH menatap jalan utara — tanah bergetar, debu meloncat, deru mesin tumbuh, yang datang BELUM terlihat)** → **`reveal` (framing SAMA, SENGAJA tanpa cut = bayaran telegraf: TANK menerobos masuk frame menuju lensa)** → `lock` (CUT ke profil samping: laras MENGAYUN ke heli sementara **pilot MENGANGKAT heli — heli mencoba kabur**) → `fire` (CUT ke two-shot lebar: railgun MENGISI DAYA lalu menembak; **`addHitStop` = GERAK LAMBAT**) → `shell` (peluru menyeberang, kamera mendorong masuk) → **`crash` (CUT tepat di HANTAMAN: `blastHelicopter` lalu bangkai JATUH dari udara & menghantam pelataran + guncangan/debu)** → `advance` (tank menggilas maju ke `BOSS_POS`, kamera mengorbit) → **`faceOff` (KARTU BOSS: hero angle rendah dari DEPAN, badan lurus & **turret BERPALING dari bangkai ke PLAYER**)** → `panBack` (fokus kembali ke player, sudut MENDARAT di `CAM_OFF_DEFAULT`) → `endCutscene`. **SINEMATOGRAFI:** `camOff`/`camTarget` + `setShotCam(az,dist,h,fogNear,fogFar)` / `shotCam(A,B,k)` / tabel `SHOT` — persis pola dua cutscene lain, dibaca renderer lewat hook `activeScene.camOffset` (stage4 mendelegasikannya); azimut ditulis `az(delta)` = simpangan dari `CAM_OFF_DEFAULT` (JANGAN salin angkanya) sehingga shot pembuka DAN penutup LITERAL = kamera gameplay → tak ada jentikan saat cutscene mulai maupun saat duel dibuka. `settleAll(dt, snap)` meredam kamera + kabut + titik fokus (satu titik terapan di akhir `runCutscene`); `cutTo(shot, fx, fz)` = **POTONGAN film** (sudut + fokus pindah dalam satu frame) — **EMPAT cut: masuk `tremorA`, `lockA`, `fireWide`, dan `impact`**. Titik fokus diredam DI SINI lalu ditulis `setCineFocus(x, z, true)` (ease 1,5/dtk milik `followViewCam` terlalu lambat & seragam untuk bahasa kamera per-shot). `faceAction(x,z)` meng-override `aimPoint` supaya AVATAR menatap aksi. Kabut per-shot disimpan di `start()` & DIPULIHKAN di `endCutscene`/`reset`. Takarir English lewat `showCineCaption` (`CAP`, 5 baris; dilepas di akhir). Lintasan masuk tank = SATU garis `TANK_FROM`(jauh di utara)→`TANK_FIRE` yang dilalui MENERUS lintas fase `tremor`+`reveal` (tanpa patahan kecepatan di batas shot); `turretForward()` = laras lurus mengikuti badan (`turretYaw = hullYaw − π/2`, kontrak tank.js). **MENEROBOS RUKO (2026-07-28, permintaan user):** lintasan masuk tank TIDAK lagi dihardcode — `TANK_FROM` DITURUNKAN dari garis `TANK_FIRE`→`deps.SMASH` (posisi ruko, stage4.`S4_SMASH`) yang diperpanjang ke utara sampai `SQ.z0 − 330`, jadi tank SELALU menabrak tepat di tengah muka bangunan walau posisinya digeser (dijaga assert properti: geser ruko 90 unit → lintasan ikut bergeser). `checkSmash()` (dipanggil di fase `tremor` DAN `reveal`) memicu `deps.smash(DRIVE_DIR)` saat moncong menyentuh muka utara (`SMASH_HIT_Z`, dihitung dari geometri — bukan dari nama fase, jadi tetap tepat kalau durasi shot di-retune) + `addCamShake(8)` + `addHitStop(0.12, 0.45)`. **Tank TIDAK melambat** saat menabrak: yang mengalah bangunannya (tank 60 ton yang tersendat justru mematahkan kesan berat) — laju masuknya tetap satu garis menerus. Dengan penempatan sekarang runtuhnya jatuh di ~82% fase `tremor`: bangunan meledak di ujung telegraf, lalu tank menerobos keluar dari debunya ke dalam frame `reveal`. **stage4 tetap yang mengurus DUEL** (updateTank/kunci arena/menang) + tetap men-`disposeTank` sebelum `intro.reset()`. Dijaga 8 assert `S4 CUTSCENE *` di smoke (urutan 11 shot, kontrak narasi, telegraf, laju masuk menerus, ragam sudut/jarak/tinggi, 4 cut + kemulusan per-frame, serah-terima di sudut gameplay, heli terangkat lalu jatuh, hit-stop, laras mengunci player, kabut dipulihkan, takarir).

- **Current override — `campaign/cutscenes/intro.js` (three-scene re-cut, 2026-08-01):** this supersedes the legacy 12-shot entry above. Exports `INTRO_DIALOGUE`, `beginIntro`, `skipIntro`, `warmupIntro`, `introDebug`, `introMetrics`, and `introScene`. Runtime order is `briefing` (scene 1, front-right close-up + exact bottom-box typewriter + configured 3-second hold) → `landing`/`exit` (scene 2, right-side close-up + slow touchdown + actual sliding door + avatar steps out) → `run`/`enter` (scene 3, front close-up + camera tracking Gibran into the rooftop door) → `stage1Scene`. The old rope/rappel/heli-departure phases are no longer reachable. Camera shots are relative to `HELI_YAW`; `warmupIntro` pre-renders the three angles. `introDebug()` now includes `{scene, dialogueChars, dialogueShown, dialogueVisible, doorOpen, heliYaw, cabinExit, roofExit}` in addition to the existing transform fields. `entities/helicopter.js` now returns `parts.rightDoor`, tracks `doorOpen`, and exports `setHelicopterDoor(heli,k)` (0 closed..1 open); Stage 4 remains closed by default.

- **campaign/cutscenes/tankBossOutro.js** — post-boss cutscene controller. Exports `TANK_BOSS_OUTRO_DIALOGUE` (five exact `{key,speaker,text}` records) and `createTankBossOutro({getTank,onComplete})` → `{start,update,reset,isActive,isDone,camOffset,cineDebug,dialogueDebug}`. Stage 4 waits for the tank's real `deathPhase==='wreck'`, then runs `tankOutro.preCutsceneDelaySec` outside the controller so camera and control remain gameplay. When the delay expires, `start()` releases held inputs, freezes controls, and opens Scene 1 on the settled wreck for `tankShotMinSec`; Scene 2 enables the avatar radio pose and runs every body through `CFG.campaign.dialogue.{cps,holdSec}`, forwarding the active dialogue key and typed fraction as gesture inputs. The final hold enters `fade` for `tankOutro.fadeSec`, then cleans camera/fade/dialogue/pose and calls `onComplete`; Stage 4 supplies a callback that opens green `STAGE 4 COMPLETE`, whose continuation opens Field Shop/Stage 5. `stage4/index.js` re-exports the script and exposes its delay/cinematic/dialogue debug helpers.
- **playerAvatar.js radio-pose additions** — `setAvatarRadioPose(on,yaw=0,gesture='gibranCall',progress=0)` and `avatarRadioDebug()`. While active, `updatePlayerAvatar` takes an early cinematic branch: root faces `yaw`, left hand is fake-IK'd to the left earpiece, right hand stays on the active weapon grip, and the weapon remains low with positive pitch so the muzzle points down. Five keyed performances animate torso/head/stance/weapon motion for the urgent call, Command listening, shocked protest, bad-news slump, and final acceptance; `progress` synchronizes the emphatic change with the typewriter. `resetAvatarDeath()` clears this state. The rig creates no new runtime meshes/materials.

## config/gameplay.json — kunci tuning

**Hack config correction (2026-08-01):** `campaign.hack.gridMin` and `gridMax` were removed and replaced by `campaign.hack.gridSize` (currently 5). This single value controls every ICE BREACH board.

**Current `campaign.intro` keys (supersedes the legacy key list still preserved in the historical row below):** `dialogueCps`, `dialogueHoldSec`, `sceneFadeSec`, `landingSec`, `doorOpenSec`, `exitSec`, `runSec`, `enterSec`. During `briefing`, the heli interpolates `BRIEF_START → LAND`; `scene1Fade` then fades out for `sceneFadeSec`, cuts to the right-side shot under black, and starts Scene 2 with a fade-in of the same configured duration.

**Current `campaign.tankOutro` keys:** `preCutsceneDelaySec` (gameplay pause after the tank first reaches `wreck`, before any cinematic freeze), `tankShotMinSec` (minimum Scene-1 wreck close-up), `radioPushSec` (duration of the slow Scene-2 camera push), and `fadeSec` (black fade before the Field Shop transition). Spoken timing remains centralized in `campaign.dialogue.{cps,holdSec}`.

**Current `campaign.stage8` keys:** opening delay/minimum/fade; `roadSpeed`; lane width/change/median-change; pickup cap/speed/wreck duration/every-N resupply and entry/approach tuning; `groundPickupTarget`, ground start/spawn timing, boss approach delay and cyclic `groundLoads[]`; gunship intro/death-delay/arrival minimum; `scenery.riceAfterFraction`; `barrelDropper.*`; and scene-local pacing. Gunship HP/score/hit/attack tuning lives only at `campaign.bosses.gunship`; no Stage 8 route-km/countdown keys remain.

**Current Stage 9–12 config:** `campaign.stage9` owns the airport encounters and takeoff pacing. `campaign.stage10` owns port pacing at its top level and Green Firewall scan/artillery/encounters under `.chapter2`; `campaign.stage11` owns Warden upload/encounters; `campaign.stage12` owns Monas return/guard/epilogue pacing. Boss tuning remains exclusively under `campaign.bosses.warden` and `.mahapatih`. Dialogue mirrors that structure: Stage 10 port lines live under `dialogue.campaign.stage10.lines`, forest lines under `.stage10.chapter2.lines`, followed by `.stage11.lines` and `.stage12.lines`.

| Seksi | Kunci |
|---|---|
| `player` | maxHp, speed, radius, **propRadius** (3.5 — radius bentrok khusus PERABOT indoor stage 1-3 lewat `propClearance()`, 2026-08-11; `radius` tetap 5 utk dinding/pintu/peti/barel/survival **dan acuan jangkauan robot**), eyeHeight, gravity (jatuh dari tepian; lompat dihapus), **medkitHealPct** (0.7 = +70% HP), **maxMedkits** (2), **medkitUseSec** (2 = detik tahan klik utk pakai medkit), **medkitSlowMul** (0.75 = gerak −25% saat memakai medkit), **deathDelaySec** (jeda sekuens kematian player sebelum layar GAME OVER, 2026-07-12; **4 dtk sejak 2026-07-26** — keruntuhannya butuh ~2,1 dtk, sisanya beat tahan), **death.{slowMoScale (0.45 = seberapa lambat dunia saat mati), slowMoHoldSec (1 = lama ditahan sebelum pulih), camZoom (0.42 = fraksi pendekatan death cam), camShake (7 = guncangan saat jasad menghantam lantai)}** (2026-07-26 — angka RASA sekuens kematian; **panjang fase & amplitudo pose sengaja TETAP di kode** [nilai visual murni]; lihat docs/presentation.md), **hpUpgrades** (tangga MAX HP item Vitality — 3 tingkat [150, 200, 250], 2026-07-15) |
| `armor` | **tiers[]** (2026-07-13: `{reduce, durability}` per tier armor shop — 0.3/100, 0.5/200, 0.7/300; `reduce` = potongan damage ke HP, durability menerima damage BASE penuh, 0 = hancur) |
| `stamina` | max, meleeCost, regenPerSec, recoverThreshold (sprintDrainPerSec & adsDrainPerSec DIHAPUS — sprint & ADS tak ada) |
| `dodge` | staminaCost (20), cooldownSec (1.0), durationSec (0.45), speed (2.2 — diturunkan dari 4.2 2026-07-12: salto pendek ~4 m) — dodge/evade Shift (2026-07-11) |

| `weapons` | **maxWeapons** (3 = slot senjata maks sejak 2026-07-11), **upgradeDamagePct** (**0.4 = +40% damage base per level upgrade shop — dinaikkan dari 0.25 pada 2026-08-13 atas permintaan user; SATU-SATUNYA pembacanya `weaponDamage()`, dan kartu shop mengutipnya sendiri sehingga retune tak perlu sentuh kode**), **maxWeaponLevel** (3 = plafon level upgrade), **ammoUpgrades** (kap peluru per senjata utk tier Ammo Capacity I/II/III — 3 tingkat, tiap tier +20% kap dasar kecuali launcher +15%; dibaca `maxAmmoFor`, 2026-07-15), bulletSpeed, bulletLife, bulletDamage (fallback), **splashRadiusMeters** (BARU 2026-08-16, permintaan user: AREA OF DAMAGE peluru BIASA — 1 m, dibaca HANYA lewat `bulletSplashRadius()` di robots.js; peluru yang mengenai robot ikut melukai robot lain dalam radius ini dari titik tumbuk, damage rata = damage peluru itu; 0/dihapus = fitur mati, launcher tetap memakai radius ledakannya sendiri `grenade.killRadius`+3.5), spreadBase, spreadBloom, heatPerShot, heatCoolPerSec, adsAccuracy (dorman — ADS dihapus; crouchAccuracy DIBUANG 2026-07-11), **`recoil.{durMul, durMin, durMax, gunPitch, gunPush, gunYaw, torso, twist, headNod, bodyDip, kneeFlex, shove, climbPerShot, climbDecayPerSec, climbPitch, camShake, flashFromKick, heavyKick, heatSmoke}` (BARU 2026-07-28 — HENTAKAN TEMBAKAN SINEMATIK; SEMUANYA dikalikan `cameraKick` senjata yang meletus, jadi satu blok ini menyetel keempat senjata sekaligus: panjang animasi dari kadens senjata [durMul/durMin/durMax], amplitudo tiap kanal badan [laras naik/mundur/menyamping, torso, bahu, kepala, badan turun, lutut, dorongan mundur], muzzle-climb rentetan [climbPerShot/climbDecayPerSec/climbPitch], guncangan kamera per tembakan [camShake], besar kilat [flashFromKick], ambang senjata BERAT yang menyemburkan asap+debu [heavyKick], dan ambang panas laras berasap [heatSmoke])**; per senjata (`rifle`/`pistol`/`shotgun`/**`launcher`**): **maxAmmo** (kap kolam peluru — rifle 500 / pistol 150 / shotgun 300 / **launcher 25**), **ammoPickup** (isi ulang per drop peluru — 80/30/50/**8**), fireDelayMs, **cameraKick** (SKALA hentakan senjata itu — dikalikan SEMUA kanal `recoil.*` di atas: pistol 0.016 mencuit, rifle 0.012 mencericit cepat, shotgun & launcher 0.03 = di atas `heavyKick` sehingga ikut menyemburkan asap + debu + dorongan badan), **damage** (dibawa peluru, DIKALI level upgrade via `weaponDamage()`; rifle 40/pistol 25/shotgun 10-per-pelet/**launcher 100 AoE**) (+ shotgun: pellets, pelletSpread; + **launcher: roundSpeed** — peluru MELEDAK saat kena, radius = `grenade.killRadius`+3.5) |

## Menjalankan & menguji

### Addendum Stage 4 boss outro (2026-08-01)

`campaign/cutscenes/tankBossOutro.js` exports `TANK_BOSS_OUTRO_DIALOGUE` plus `createTankBossOutro({getTank,onComplete})`. Stage 4 re-exports the script and exposes `outroDelayDebug()`/`outroCineDebug()`/`outroDialogueDebug()`. Tank death finishes its animation and remains in gameplay for `tankOutro.preCutsceneDelaySec` after reaching `wreck`; only then does Scene 1 begin and freeze input. The controller cuts to five keyed, continuously animated avatar radio performances synchronized to their shared-typewriter bodies, fades black, cleans every cinematic override, and invokes Stage 4's callback. That callback invokes `beginStageTransition(stage5Scene)`, which opens `STAGE 4 COMPLETE`; only its continuation opens Field Shop. Its camera override has priority over `tankBossIntro.camOffset()` while active. `playerAvatar.js` adds `setAvatarRadioPose(on,yaw,gesture,progress)` and `avatarRadioDebug()`; reset clears the pose.

### Addendum Stage 1 radio (2026-08-01)

- `campaign/stages/stage1/index.js` kini mengekspor `S1_RADIO_DIALOGUE`; alur sukses hack yang kanonik adalah `downloading → radio → clear2`, bukan langsung `clear2`. Fase `radio` mengetik body Pilot lalu Maj. Gibran huruf-per-huruf, membekukan kontrol, dan menunda `spawnWave2()` + `spawnStage1Horde()` sampai kedua baris selesai. `s1Debug()` kini juga memuat `radioIndex`, `radioT`, dan `radioChars`.
- `core/dom.js` mengekspor `showStageRadioDialogue(speaker,text,typing)`, `hideStageRadioDialogue()`, dan `stageRadioDialogueDebug()` untuk panel `#stageRadioDialogue` di `index.html`; `typing=true` menyalakan caret typewriter.
- Tuning typewriter bersama: `CFG.campaign.dialogue.{cps,holdSec}`. Stage 1 dan Stage 2 membaca sumber yang sama; smoke mematok naskah persis, progres huruf-per-huruf, urutan speaker, hold config-driven, kondisi tanpa wave selama dialog Stage 1, serta cleanup panel/kontrol.
- `campaign/stages/stage2/index.js` mengekspor `S2_DIALOGUE` dan `s2DialogueDebug()`. Empat key satu-kali (`stageStart`, `liftDead`, `inspectGenerator`, `generatorRestored`) masuk antrean typewriter kontekstual; antrean mencegah event baru memotong body aktif, sementara `seen` mencegah dialog proximity berulang.

### Addendum Stage 3 dialogue (2026-08-01)

`campaign/stages/stage3/index.js` exports `S3_DIALOGUE` and `s3DialogueDebug()`. Five exact Major Gibran lines are queued at stage entry, first successful hack, **last** successful hack (the `hackRequired`-th, 3 since 2026-08-13), lobby entry, and proximity to a locked/red terminal — the last of which now also covers the two terminals that were left out of this run's random subset. All bodies use the shared `CFG.campaign.dialogue.{cps,holdSec}` typewriter. Locked-terminal dialogue re-arms after leaving its range; story lines remain one-shot. `exit()` hides the shared radio panel during ICE BREACH without discarding its typewriter/queue state.

### Addendum Stage 5 depot combat pass (2026-08-08)

This supersedes the older Stage-5 statements that `robotStationWalk`/clamp reject SA/S. `campaign/stages/stage5/world.js` now exports `S5_SPAWN_MACHINE`, `S5_MACHINE_SPAWNS`, `BARREL_POINTS`, `safeDoor()`, `updateStationSpawnMachine()` and `killStationSpawnMachine()`. SA/S reject spawn selection only; living robots may path and clamp into them after the safe door starts opening, and `updateStationDoors(dt, platformOpen, safeOpen)` latches that door open for depot combat. The shared hero rig is a solid/nav-baked central objective; `station.js` applies swept bullet damage and runs the config-driven charge → staggered materialize/eject → landing sequence, holding each robot’s AI until landing. C1 progression requires the machine and all robots destroyed. `stage5WorldDebug()` exposes the rig, hatch points, Stage-1/2-compatible yellow C1/C2 stand boxes, furniture and barrels; `stage5Debug().machine` exposes HP/cycle/batch/birth state. `CFG.campaign.stage5.spawnMachine` owns `hitRadius`, `batchCount`, `batchSec`, `chargeSec`, `birthGapSec`, `birthSec` and normalized `classMix`; its HP comes from the shared `CFG.campaign.spawnMachine.hp`. Six additional nav-baked furniture islands and eight shared explosive barrels fill the warehouse; smoke separately pathfinds safe-area/C1/C2 routes.

### Addendum shared split-door rig (2026-08-08)

This addendum also supersedes the older Stage 5/6 fixed-whole-doorway collision wording in the module catalog: active doors use the Stage 1 timing, proximity/linger, `open < 0.5` solid threshold, and moving-leaf bullet sweep/clamp.

This addendum supersedes the older downward/upward door-animation wording in the catalog above. `campaign/utility/doors.js` now exports `buildSplitDoor(parent, material, x, y, z, sx, sy, sz)`, `setSplitDoorOpen(rig, easedOpen)`, and `splitDoorDebug(rig)`. Every active campaign door consists of two equal 50:50 leaves that move symmetrically left/right along its wall: Stage 1-3 automatic doors, Stage 3 blast + exit doors, Stage 5 station doors, and both Stage 6 chapters. Stage 1 is the behavior standard: `doorProximityTarget()` provides the 2.5-cell front zone plus configured close-delay linger, `updateDoorMotion()` provides the 0.45 s exact-settle quadratic easing, `resolveDoors()` uses the shared `open < 0.5` solid threshold, and `doorBlocksShot()`/`doorClampShot()` follow the two moving leaf rectangles so the visible leaves and bullet collision cannot disagree. Broken/jammed doors and road bollards are outside this active-door rig. Smoke asserts the two leaves, symmetry, fixed vertical position, stage wiring, and full collision/SFX behavior.

### Addendum door-side indicators (2026-08-11)

`buildDoorSideLights()` in `campaign/utility/doors.js` gives custom Stage 5 and Stage 6 doors the same four small status strips as Stage 1: two opposing jambs, visible from both wall faces, at mid-wall height. An accessible door is always green, even while closed; a locked or sealed door is always red. The former single horizontal lamp above each Stage 5/6 door is removed; door state recolors all four strips together. Smoke checks their jamb-side positions, colors, and non-overhead placement.

### Addendum robot no-path + dynamic-door navigation (2026-08-11)

`utils/pathfind.js` accepts an optional dynamic walk predicate in `gridLOS`, `findPath` and `navAim`. `navAim` now returns the shared `{x,z,direct,reachable}`; a blocked target with no A* route reports `reachable:false` instead of the former straight-chase fallback. `campaign/utility/doors.js` exports `doorsWalkable(doors,x,z,radius)`, a non-mutating predicate using the same footprint and `open < 0.5` threshold as `resolveDoors`. Stage 1–3, Stage 5 station and both Stage 6 chapters pass it as `pathWalkable`; Stage 5–6 also pass wall-plus-door bullet LOS to `campaignRobotAI`. When no route exists, `campaignRobotAI` sets `navIdle`, suppresses movement/aim/attack, and `entities/robots.js` continues the class-specific idle rig while logical state remains `chasing` for repath. A/B inside normal activation distance may wake without initial LOS specifically to seek a firing position. The same no-route idle behavior applies to melee campaign robots.

### Addendum Stage 5–13 location-sign removal (2026-08-11; extended 2026-08-13)

Stage 5–12 build no physical place-name, destination, wayfinding, gantry, terminal-name, airport-name, billboard, shop-name, or landmark-plaque boards. `entities/train.js` owns the Stage 5 journey/arrival scenery portion; Stage 6 removes boards from both chapters; Stage 7–8 remove flyover/highway/airport labels; Stage 9–12 communicate Kertajati, the logistics coast, Kalimantan, IKN and Jakarta only through authored environment and architecture. Gameplay markers, terminal displays, railway/traffic signals and door-status jamb lights remain. Smoke scans canonical builders so a location board cannot silently return.

`campaign/cutscenes/tankBossIntro.js` exports `TANK_BOSS_DIALOGUE`; Stage 4 re-exports it plus `tankDialogueDebug()`. The four exact typewriter beats are ring-triggered heli arrival (Pilot), tank reveal (Pilot), distorted radio cutoff (Pilot), and post-explosion vow (Major Gibran). `start()` first freezes control (`releaseInputs` + `cinematicActive`), then queues the empty LZ body; no dialogue appears merely because the heli landed. `survey` waits for that first line, the cutoff is interrupted by the actual `blastHelicopter` beat, and `panBack` waits for the final reaction before enabling battle. `core/dom.js` accepts optional `distorted` in `showStageRadioDialogue(speaker,text,typing,distorted=false)` and clears that class/dataset in `hideStageRadioDialogue()`.

### Addendum Stage 1 layout revision (2026-08-12; supersedes the Stage 1 door/flow wording above)

`campaign/stages/stage1/index.js` now follows the user's revised `stages(Stage1).csv`. `S1_MAP` still holds walls only; every other legend token is realized by its own table, so the grid BFS/no-double-wall invariants are unchanged:

- **`+` broken door** — `S1_DOORS` entries carrying `broken: true` (start↔office W at c3-4 r7 and conference W↔central hall at c13-15 r7). `campaign/utility/doors.js` gained `DOOR_BROKEN_AJAR` (0.14): a broken door is forced locked, is pinned once at build time to that jammed opening, is skipped entirely by `updateStageDoors` (leaves never move, no SFX), and `setDoorLocked()` refuses to unlock it. Below `DOOR_SOLID_MAX`, so `resolveDoors`/`doorsWalkable`/`doorBlocksShot` treat it as solid for player, robots and bullets.
- **`/` wall breach** — `S1_BREACHES` (`s1BreachesDbg()`): c39 r3 (supply room W↔east-1) and c28 r7 (conference E↔toilet). The cell is plain floor; only jagged jamb stubs are added, deliberately without blockers because a player of radius 5 can never come within 2 units of a cell edge.
- **`*` furniture pile** — `S1_BARRICADES` (`s1BarricadesDbg()`, 29 cells: r13 c9-20, c39 r18-24, r25 c30-39). One full-cell non-standable blocker per cell plus a pile drawn from **eight recipes** (`BARRICADE_PILES`, added on the user's "monoton" report): crate tower, filing cupboards toppled over a desk, lounge sofa wedged with crates, upended meeting table, stacked toilet partitions, cupboard row with rubble, wrecked console + bench, concrete planter + crates. Recipe, 90° spin and jitter come from a deterministic hash of the cell index (never `Math.random()` — `buildWorld` runs during loading beside every other campaign world). Nav is baked from the blockers, so robots route around them. `s1BarricadeMixDbg()` reports the recipe/kinds actually used.
- **`@` computer bank + `$` stand cell** — `S1_TERMINAL_BANK` (c48 r30-38) is a solid mainframe run along the east wall of the access room, hand-built rather than reusing a cupboard (the user's "placeholder" report): per cell a plinthed cabinet pushed back to the wall with an open face carrying four alternating-depth blade rows with activity lamps, vent louvres, a status-LED column and a framed screen, plus a steel crown and a cable trunk above. The `$` row (r34) gets an operator station — large tilted screen, protruding keyboard shelf, closed access panel. **Every part stays inside cell 48**, because the cell west of it is where the player stands. PAL tokens only, emissive ≤ `EMISSIVE_MAX`, zero PointLights, all routed through `addMergedStatic`; `s1BankDbg()` exposes the parts.
- **NAC door** — the c39 r43-45 locked door is now released by the `@` bank.

The phase machine **starts at `access`** — the old `clear1` "destroy every robot to proceed" gate was removed on the user's request the same day, so the wave-1 garrison can simply be walked past. Standing on `$` (`CFG.campaign.stage1.computerRange`, **no minigame** — the user's rule) unlocks the NAC door; only `clear2`, after the download, still requires a clear floor before the stairs accept the player. Stage 1 now uses the shared `buildStandMarker`/`pulseStandMarker` from `campaign/utility/common.js` for **two** action markers and, like Stage 6, lights exactly one at a time, always matching what the radar points at (`s1MarkersDbg()`); the `access` marker is lit from build time. Other new/changed doors: c8 r24-25 (office SW↔small room 1) and the access-room door narrowed to c39 r31-32. New exports: `S1_ACCESS`, `S1_TERMINAL_BANK`, `s1DoorsDbg()`, `s1BarricadesDbg()`, `s1BreachesDbg()`, `s1MarkersDbg()`, `s1BankDbg()`, `s1BarricadeMixDbg()`; `s1Debug()` gains `nacLocked`.

Smoke additions (section 16b-2): all 29 barricade cells stay floor in the grid yet reject the player and are absent from the nav grid; both breaches admit a full-radius player on the cell and on both sides; both broken doors stay locked, never move and resist `setDoorLocked`; the bank is solid, `$` is standable, the bank carries ≥15 parts per cell with real (non-black) emissive screens and a tilted operator screen while **no part leaves cell 48** (rotated x-span measured); the barricades use ≥6 recipes over ≥7 furniture kinds with the longest line varied; and a quarter-cell clearance BFS proves every room, every openable door mouth and every objective point still reachable from START through the real furniture. The flow test now proves the NAC door opens while the whole wave-1 garrison is still alive. The no-double-wall detector skips corner nubs (a wall cell that is a lone nub in its own row/column is not a parallel wall run) so the c39 r30 wall corner is not a false positive.

### Addendum shared barricade/breach + Stage 2 layout revision (2026-08-13)

**`campaign/utility/barricade.js`** (new) owns the CSV legend tokens `'*'` and `'/'` for every stage that adopts them, so the two can never fork per stage. Exports: `BARRICADE_TOP` (14), `BARRICADE_PILES` (the eight recipes), `barricadeBlocker(x, z, cell)` → one full-cell `standable:false` blocker for the stage's `blockers` array (nav is baked from it, so robots route around), `buildFurniturePile(out, x, z, seed)` → pushes a pile into the stage's `staticProps` and returns `{recipe, kinds}`, and `buildWallBreach(out, x, z, dir, cell, wallH)` → jagged jamb stubs only, deliberately blocker-free (a radius-5 player can never reach within 2 units of a cell edge). Recipe, 90° spin and jitter come from a deterministic hash of the cell index — never `Math.random()`, because every stage's `buildWorld` runs during loading and would shift other stages' random placement. Stage 1 was refactored onto it the same day.

**Stage 2** (`campaign/stages/stage2/index.js`) now follows the user's revised `stages(Stage2).csv` under the same rule that `S2_MAP` holds only walls. New: `S2_BREACHES` (`s2BreachesDbg()`, one cell — c38 r6, supply room → toilet), `S2_BARRICADES` (`s2BarricadesDbg()`, 53 cells — r9 c9-29 and c42-43 r13-28), `s2BarricadeMixDbg()`, the c6-7 r9 door carrying `broken: true`, and a new door at c8 r11-12. Two routing facts are load-bearing: with row 9 sealed and its door dead, **the c38 r6 breach is the only way down from the upper floor** (supply → toilet → c30 r11-12 → central hall → the new c8 door); and the c42-43 column makes **c40-41 the only path from the c39 r27-28 door** into the rest of the generator room, so that lane was emptied (five pieces moved to c44-48). Rows 7-8 became a two-cell corridor, so three row-8 pieces moved down into the hall. A **third** routing fact was missed on the first pass and cost a softlock (user report the same day): the **c8-11 stretch of that corridor is the only exit from the start room**, because the c8 wall closes rows 1-6 and the c6-7 r9 door is broken — the 34×18 `meeting` table at c10 r7 sealed it, so it is deleted.

**Locked-door player collision (2026-08-13 bugfix).** `resolveDoors`/`doorsWalkable`/`doorClampShot` stop robots and bullets, but the player is stopped only where the scene's `playerCollide` calls `resolveDoors(doors, pos, player.radius, true)`. Stage 2 never had a locked door before the CSV revision, so it had never wired that call and the new broken door was walk-through for the player alone; it now matches Stage 1. Smoke drives the real `playerCollide` at every broken door in both stages and requires a push-out (mutation-verified).

Smoke: a Stage 2 mirror of section 16b-2 — the 53 barricade cells stay floor in the grid yet reject the player and vanish from the nav grid; the breach admits a full-radius player on the cell and both sides; the broken door stays locked, never moves and resists `setDoorLocked`; the piles use ≥6 recipes over ≥7 furniture kinds; and a quarter-cell clearance BFS proves every room, every openable door mouth and every objective still reachable from START through the real furniture. The Stage 2 door-mouth list gains `[8,11]`, `[8,12]` and `[38,6]`.

**Clearance BFS must block BROKEN doors (2026-08-13 bugfix #2).** Both stages' clearance BFS filtered only through `stageNWalk` + `resolve` — walls and furniture, **no doors at all** — so it walked through the broken `+` door and pronounced the map fully connected while the player was actually walled into Stage 2's start room. The sample predicate now also calls `resolveDoors(doors.filter(d => d.broken), pos, PRAD, true)`, in the same order `playerCollide` uses. Filter to `broken` only: a plain `locked` door such as Stage 1's NAC is opened by the objective flow and is not a permanent wall. Mutation-verified — restoring the deleted table drops Stage 2's reach to 618/23409 and flags every objective and door mouth as walled off.

Drop animates whole-rig altitude, spin, tilt and squash; unfold separately expands the existing frame/chamber/gantry/hatch/crown/turbines. **Stage 10 Chapter 1→2 handoff, forest terrain and boundary fence (2026-08-26, user report):** four changes. (1) **The chapter handoff is ASYNC behind the shared loading screen.** `enterStage10Chapter2()` (`stage10ChapterHandoff()` returns the pending promise, `null` otherwise; `stage10Debug().chapterHandoff` mirrors it) now calls `setPaused(true)` + `showLoading()`, then splits the work across `await loadingStep(...)` boundaries so the browser paints between chunks: dispose every Chapter 1 robot and transient entity array → `stage10ForestScene.enter()` → `renderer.compile` + three real composer frames → `HANDOFF_MIN_MS` (900) hold → `hideLoading()` + `setPaused(false)`. Pointer lock is never released, so no `pointerlockchange` fires, no pause menu appears and play resumes without a click. It used to run synchronously mid-frame from `finishExtraction`, freezing the image for hundreds of ms with no feedback. Any caller that needs the new chapter must await the promise. (2) **`stage10ForestWalk`'s boundary is marked by a real FENCE.** `buildBoundaryFence()` traces the walkable predicate with marching squares (`FENCE_STEP` 20, crossings found by bisection so the contour is smooth, not a staircase) and lays a continuous concrete kerb + steel posts + two rails + periodic hazard plates on it. It is **purely a marker — no `addBoxBlocker`, no `addTrunk`** — and panels whose segment already intersects a blocker or trunk are skipped so the fence never runs through a sensor mast, crest cover or tree. `stage10ForestWorldDebug().fence` exposes `{step, top, segments, posts}`. (3) **The service road is ONE mitered ribbon.** `miterFrames(pts)` + `ribbon(parent, mat, frames, half, off, y)` build the road, its gravel shoulder and both drainage channels as single `ShapeGeometry` polygons with **per-vertex** widths, replacing per-segment boxes that each used `min(a.w, b.w)` and their own yaw — the source of the width steps and outer-bend notches the user reported. `stage10ForestWorldDebug().road` exposes both edge polylines for the continuity assert. (4) **Vegetation species are rebuilt.** `frondAngles(A, T)`/`frond(...)` map a leaf direction through euler `(0, ry, rz)`, because with the XYZ order the old `rotation.set(PI/2, a, …)` made the Y term an *inner* rotation that never touched the local +Y axis — every palm and tree-fern frond on a tree pointed at world +Z and speared its own trunk, while `scale.z` shortened the cone instead of flattening it. Fronds now grow from the crown outward with a species droop and a real blade cross-section; palms gained a crown boss and upright young fronds, bananas broad thin leaves, bamboo slender ringed culms with small leaf sprays (replacing 2 m cones that read as party hats), tree ferns their own short trunk, and dipterocarps supporting branches plus a flattened five-blob canopy. Per-species heights replaced the shared 2.8 factor.

The facade holds it there — `stage10ForestScene.robotAI` passes `activate: (z,d) => ambushReleased && d < activateMeters·CAMP_M` — until the player advances `safeStartMeters` from the start, at which point `updateProgression` sets `ambushReleased`, calls `activateStage10ForestPrefix('forest-0')`, queues the `ambushSprung` line and shows a contact banner. `stage10Debug().safeStart` exposes `{x, z, radius, released, advance}`.

Three non-overlapping RTGs own three 50-unit local routes. `syncLiftRig` centers each trolley over its assigned container and joins trolley→cables→spreader→container throughout the eased lift/travel/lower curve; `trolleyWithinGantry` rejects the former floating-trolley pose. Each RTG/QCC static root uses `weldOccluder`, and each trolley is a dynamic `registerOccluder`, so crane pieces use the same configured campaign fade as other tall props. `craneDebug()` exposes gantry footprints/separation/fade readiness plus per-container alignment, attachment, cable extension, in-frame state, and crane identity.

### Addendum shared blocker index + per-stage world roots (2026-08-13)

**`utils/collision.js` gained `makeBlockerIndex(blockers, { cell, x0, z0 })`** — the uniform-grid blocker index built for Stage 6 HQ, promoted to the shared layer and adopted by Stages 1, 2 and HQ itself (its private copy is gone). Returns `{ rebuild(), gather(x, z, radius, moving = true), debug() }`. `rebuild()` spreads every blocker over all grid cells its AABB touches, stamps each with its position in the original array, and records the largest half-edge as the query margin; it must be re-run whenever the blocker list changes. `gather()` returns only the blockers near a point, **re-sorted into original list order** and — for `moving:true`, i.e. anything that feeds `resolveBlockers`, which shifts the point as it iterates — widened by that margin, so the result is byte-identical to a full sweep. Point queries (`blockersGroundHeight`) pass `moving:false`. `debug()` reports `{cells, blockers, cell, pad}`.

**Every campaign stage world now has a registered root.** `registerCampaignWorldRoot`/`setActiveCampaignWorldRoots` covers `campaign-1` … `campaign-8`, the extra Stage 6 HQ root, Stage 9's interior/runway roots, Stage 10's port/forest roots, Stage 11's surface/root pair, and final `campaign-12`. `activeCampaignWorldRoots()` returns the active key list. `core/sceneManager.js` maps facade ids `campaign-10`→`campaign-10-port` and `campaign-11`→`campaign-11-surface`; modal/shop/menu ids leave root visibility untouched. Stages 6, 9, 10, and 11 select their internal chapter root from sub-scene `enter()`.

Supporting changes: `buildStageDoors(doorList, cellFn, CELL, H, parent)` takes an optional parent so a stage's doors and jamb lamps ride its world root (default stays `scene`); new debug getters `s1WorldRootDbg`/`s2WorldRootDbg`/`s3WorldRootDbg`/`s4WorldRootDbg`/`stage5WorldRootDbg`/`stage6WorldRootDbg`/`hqWorldRootDbg`/`stage7WorldRootDbg`, plus `s1BlockersDbg`/`s2BlockersDbg`/`hqBlockersDbg` and `s1BlockerIdxDbg`/`s2BlockerIdxDbg`.

Smoke section **26** pins all of it: index results identical to a full sweep over a 20,000-point grid in both stages (resolve *and* ground height), the index grid actually populated with a non-zero margin, the margin applied only to moving queries, every stage/chapter root registered, only the active stage's root visible (objects inside registered roots drop from ~47,000 to ~7,300 while Stage 1 is played), switching stages moving that visibility, a modal/shop scene leaving it untouched, and Stage 1's room lamps still parented to `scene` rather than the root. Stage 6's older "every prop ejects a probe" assert became a full-sweep parity assert for the same reason.


### Addendum shared occlusion fade — Stages 1–12 (2026-08-13)

**New module `src/scenes/campaign/utility/occlusion.js` is the ONE occluder-fade system for the whole campaign.** It replaces three divergent copies: Stage 4's line-of-sight test and the old forest/Monas distance-only tests. Every current Stage 1–12 world follows the shared contract.

Exports:

- `registerOccluder(key, obj, opts)` — `opts` = `{ x, z, radius, top, clone = true, dynamic = false, offX, offZ }`. `x`/`z`/`radius`/`top` default to the object's world bounding box. Materials are **cloned per occluder** so fading one prop never fades a batch that happens to share a material; the clones are made at world-build time, before the loading screen ends, so the "no mid-game shader recompile" invariant holds (`transparent`/`opacity` are not part of the r128 program cache key).
- `weldOccluder(key, parent, obj, opts)` — shorthand for props that would otherwise go into a big static batch: `mergeObjectInPlace(obj)` (keeps the object's own transform and material instances), `parent.add(...)`, then register. Costs a handful of draw calls per prop instead of dozens of loose meshes.
- `updateStageOccluders(key, dt)` — call once per frame from the stage's `updateMode`.
- `resetStageOccluders(key)` — back to opaque; call from `enter()`/world reset.
- `clearStageOccluders(key)` — drop the whole set (only needed where a world builder is exported and can run twice, i.e. Stage 1/2 under the smoke harness).
- `occlusionDebug(key)` → `{ count, faded, minFactor, opacity, maxTop, cell, points[] }`, `occlusionOpacity()`, `occlusionKeys()`.

**The test is line-of-sight with a HALF-BODY threshold, not distance** (threshold added 2026-08-14 on user request). `slope = CAM_OFF.y / |CAM_OFF.xz|` comes from the new `camOffsetActive()` export in `core/renderer.js`, so a stage with its own `camOffset` (3/7/8) automatically uses its own angle. The entity→camera ray is intersected with the occluder's axis-aligned footprint (2-D slab test, box widened by `lateralPad`), and the hidden height at the entity is `cover = (top − feetY) − slope · tEnter`. The prop fades only when `cover ≥ bodyHeight × coverFraction` — half the character by default; anything that merely covers the legs stays opaque. `bodyHeight` derives from `CFG.player.eyeHeight` unless overridden, and a robot's threshold scales with its mesh scale. The slab test (rather than "distance to centre minus half-extent") matters at this tightness for two reasons: a long vehicle occludes from its *rear face*, and a large hollow building (Stage 10's 180-unit warehouse shell) must NOT count as covering someone standing 100 units to the side — the ray never enters its footprint. The player point comes from `camera.position` (the logic pivot) and every robot within `robotRange` is swept too — that is the "player **or** robot" half of the contract.

**Cost.** Static occluders are indexed into a uniform grid once (cell `gridCell`); each frame only the cells inside the entity→camera corridor are walked, and the corridor length is capped by the tallest occluder in that stage. Moving props (`dynamic: true` — the port crane's carried containers) are kept out of the grid, have their `x`/`z` re-read each frame and their `top` raised by their lift height, and are tested directly.

**Config `campaign.occlusion`:** `opacity` (0.2 — the single fade value, never hardcode it in a test), `coverFraction` (0.5 — the half-body threshold), `bodyHeight` (0 = derive from `CFG.player.eyeHeight`), `fadeRate`, `robotRange`, `lateralPad`, `gridCell`.

### Addendum fadeable walls (2026-08-14)

**New module `src/scenes/campaign/utility/wallFade.js`** makes `#` wall cells fade like any other occluder, on the user's request that walls covering the character go transparent too. Walls are the hardest geometry to fade per-cell: Stages 1–3 put every cell in ONE `InstancedMesh` (one material — fading it fades the whole building), and Stages 5/6 melted body *and* face dressing into the shared `addMergedStatic` batch, which cannot give a cell back.

`buildFadeableWalls({ key, parent, cells, cell, wallH, bodyMat, colorAt, detail, poolSize })`:

- builds the wall bodies as one `InstancedMesh` (unchanged cost) and, when `detail` is given, the `buildDetailedWallCell` skin as **instanced meshes per (face direction × material)** — the skin geometry is identical for every exposed face, so it is recorded once on a canonical cell and instanced. That is what lets the skin disappear together with its cell; without it a translucent proxy would stand behind still-opaque panels.
- keeps a **pool of proxy cells** (`poolSize` 12). A fading cell is hidden from its instances (matrix scaled to zero) and drawn by a pooled stand-alone mesh carrying **its own cloned materials**, so every cell eases independently. Proxy materials are created at world-build time — `renderer.compile` walks the whole scene including hidden objects, so the no-mid-game-recompile rule holds.
- returns `{ body, dressing, proxies, cells, details, debug() }`; `debug()` reports `{ cells, details, dressingMeshes, pool, active, opacity, drawGroups }` and `drawGroups` is guarded at ≤ 17 (1 body + ≤ 16 skin) per stage.

**The show/hide matrix must be re-identified before reuse.** `setPosition` only touches the translation column, so reusing the same `Matrix4` that was just zero-scaled to hide a cell would restore it at scale 0 — the wall would vanish permanently. `placeMatrix()` calls `identity()` first; the smoke `Matrix4`/`InstancedMesh` stubs now record scale and position so this is a real assert (mutation-tested).

Adopted by Stage 1 (452 cells), 2 (386), 3 (293), 5 station (175), 6 Arrival (385) and 6 HQ (412). Stage 5's `@` windowed wall cells are welded occluders instead (their glass pane stays a separate transparent mesh). Stage 12's root chamber registers its 20 buttresses.

**Adoption per stage** (counts from `OCC_REPORT=1 node tools/smoke.mjs`): 1 = 29 barricade piles, 2 = 53 piles, 3 = 2 factory machines, 4 = 81 cars/roadside buildings, 5 = 25 depot/platform furniture + generator + C1 terminal + spawn machine, 6 Arrival = 20 shelves/benches/panels/racks/generators/machines, 6 HQ = 30 `C` server banks + 2 fabricators, 7 = all 240 road vehicles, 9 = tower/hangar/ops building/12 ground-support vehicles/6 masts/12 pallets, 10 = 34 container stacks + forklifts + reach stacker + warehouse shell + 30 pipe-rack bents + 5 process tanks + 3 crane-carried containers, 11 = 22 hero trees + 2 shelters + carrier wreck + 8 sensor masts + intake house, 12 = 38 covers/colonnade (surface) + 12 capacitor banks (root), 13 = 10 rain trees + 28 stalled vehicles + Monas. **Stage 8 registers nothing on purpose**: all of its scenery lives beyond |z| 96 while the player is locked to the carriageways at |z| ≲ 44, so no static geometry ever enters the sight corridor.

Supporting changes: `buildFurniturePile(out, x, z, seed, onGroup)` (utility/barricade.js) takes an optional callback that receives the pile as its own Group instead of pushing pieces into the stage batch; Stage 5's `buildGenerator`/`buildTerminal` now return their `group`; Stage 7's per-125 m vehicle merge became a **per-vehicle** weld (finer frustum culling, and the only way one bus can fade alone) while `vehicleChunkStats` keeps reporting the same binning plus `perVehicleOccluders`; Stage 10's containers/forklifts/reach stacker/warehouse shell/pipe rack/process tanks and Stage 11's sensor masts/intake house were lifted out of their shared batches.

Smoke section **27** pins it: opacity comes from CFG, no other file keeps an occluder list, the module reads the active camera slope and sweeps robots, every campaign world registers occluders, Stage 7 has exactly one occluder per vehicle, a prop fades to exactly `CFG.campaign.occlusion.opacity` for the player **and** for a robot with the player far away, recovers to opaque, resets on stage reset, and a prop outside the sight corridor stays opaque. Stage 11's old distance-based assert was rewritten onto the shared model.

- **Wajib HTTP server** (ES modules + fetch config tidak jalan di `file://`): `python -m http.server 8000` → `http://localhost:8000`. `index.html` menampilkan pesan error yang jelas bila dibuka via file://.
- `package.json` hanya metadata `{"type":"module"}` untuk tooling Node (`node --check src/**/*.js`) — tetap TANPA dependensi/build.

dibuka selama itu, lalu siap lagi setelah horde dibereskan + player menjauh + cooldown habis**)**, **MINIGAME PERBAIKAN GENERATOR "FIELD REPAIR" 2026-07-29 (SEMUA papan — 3 tipe × 3 difficulty, puluhan papan per kombinasi — DIJAMIN bisa diselesaikan dgn prosedur pemain yang wajar & tak pernah terbuka dalam keadaan sudah selesai; jumlah elemen = `CFG.campaign.repair.count[difficulty]`; kabel beda warna DITOLAK [pilihan bertahan] & klik ulang melepas; ukuran chip semuanya unik + soket salah ukuran menolak; **SERET: separuh papan kabel & chip diselesaikan lewat jalur seret saja, aturan tolaknya sama, chip diseret ke BAKI = dicabut, dan uji EVENT DOM NYATA [mini-DOM perekam sementara: mousedown→mousemove→mouseup benar-benar menyambung kabel / mendudukkan chip / mencabutnya, sementara tekan-lepas TANPA gerak BUKAN seret]**; katup BERGIGI hanya ke KANAN [dasar prosedur kiri→kanan]; tombol COLOR MODE menukar palet aman buta warna & tersimpan; modal = scene `campaign-repair` dgn `shopActive()` true, menelan tombol gameplay, tak bisa dibuka dobel, `startIndex` melanjutkan komponen, ESC = ABORT, dan TANPA timer/jalur kalah; integrasi STAGE 2 [injak marker → modal terbuka & game di-pause → papan 1 selesai = 1/3 tercatat & papan 2 muncul → ABORT menyimpan kemajuan + pemicu mati sampai player menjauh → kembali LANJUT dari komponen 2 → 3 papan selesai = wave-2 muncul & lift berdaya])**. **ATURAN: assertion HARUS config-driven** (baca `cfgMod.CFG`, jangan hardcode angka tuning — user sering me-retune gameplay.json; test gagal setelah retune murni config ≈ test-nya yang hardcode). Method stub yang kurang (fakeEl/THREE) = celah harness, bukan bug game — lengkapi stub-nya. **Alur kerja tiap perubahan gameplay: tambah/ubah assert → `node tools/smoke.mjs` (semua pass) → `node --check` file yang disentuh → sinkronkan CLAUDE.md + MODULES.md.**
